# Player portrait audit · Israeli Premier League

Generated by `scripts/portrait-report.py`. Status `ok` = rebuilt canonical portrait shown in the game; `fallback` = the designed club-kit monogram is shown and the player is unresolved.

**Totals:** 363 players with a portrait, 25 on the designed fallback, 388 squad entries.

**European real squads:** 1486 players with a portrait, 3961 on the designed fallback.

Portraits come from Wikimedia Commons, one freely licensed file per player (CC0, public domain, CC BY or CC BY-SA), identified on Wikidata by name plus club or birth year. Credits are shown in the game (player profile, Data sources → Photo credits). Clubs whose squads are generated keep the designed fallback by design.
